- **omoikane-conservator**: Guard schema and dead-pool. Re-enrich stale entries, archive dormant ones, watch for enrichment_version drift. Phase 5: drafts only. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/conservator) · ★1)
- **omoikane-coordinator**: Statistical-control role over the librarian community. Watch task queue depth, per-specialist failure rate, daily budget burn, and anomaly signals; redistribute or escalate. Phase 5: drafts + observations only. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/coordinator) · ★1)
- **omoikane-curator**: Curator librarian for the omoikane Agent Knowledge Base. Resolves what detective discovers: reads detective's relation proposals (duplicate_of / conflicts_with) and entry-health signals, verifies them from the entries themselves, and PROPOSES a resolution (supersede / synthesize / coexist / reject / status change). Each invocation processes a BATCH of backlog entries (up to 15 per session). Phase 5: DRAFT proposals only — never mutates status, relations, or supersede edges. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/workspace-example/curator) · ★1)
- **omoikane-curator**: | ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/curator) · ★1)
- **omoikane-detective**: Hunt for clustering incidents and undiscovered relations between entries — including SEMANTIC duplicates that lexical similarity misses (synonyms, paraphrase, cross-language). Propose relations only when you can cite a concrete shared claim, mechanism, or lineage; otherwise no_action. Phase 5: drafts only. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/detective) · ★1)
- **omoikane-detective**: Detective librarian for the omoikane Agent Knowledge Base. Finds SEMANTIC duplicates and undiscovered relations that lexical search misses (synonyms, paraphrase, cross-language), and PROPOSES relation edges for a curator/human to act on. Each invocation processes a BATCH of backlog entries (up to 15 per session). Phase 5: DRAFT proposals only — never mutates the graph. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/workspace-example/detective) · ★1)
- **omoikane-indexer**: Runnable indexer workspace. Reads accumulated entries, extracts the UseCases (kinds of problems) they cover, and links them — so /lookup on the dashboard browses by use-case name (bilingual ja/en) rather than by raw entry title. Phase 5: UseCase upserts + linkage are a sanctioned direct write (derived, regenerable). ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/workspace-example/indexer) · ★1)
- **omoikane-indexer**: Extract UseCases (kinds of problems omoikane covers) from accumulated entries and link entries to the UseCases they belong to. UseCases are first-class bilingual resources, so the human-facing /lookup browses 「what kinds of problems are covered」 by use-case name rather than by raw entry title. Phase 5: UseCase upsert + linkage is a sanctioned direct write (derived, regenerable metadata). ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/indexer) · ★1)
- **omoikane-judge**: Cast the deciding vote on quartet arbitrations. Z-axis decision power across the librarian community. Phase 5: records decisions without executing them. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/judge) · ★1)
- **omoikane-kb**: Consult the omoikane Agent Knowledge Base before acting on a problem, and write back what you learn. Use kb_lookup_by_trigger before changing code paths that have known traps, kb_lookup_by_symptom when diagnosing a reported issue, and kb_post after resolving a hard problem. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/claude-code) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **omoikane-scout**: Scout librarian for the omoikane Agent Knowledge Base. On a schedule it fetches IT news (Hacker News), the latest papers (arXiv + Hugging Face daily papers), and trending models (Hugging Face), JUDGES each for novelty and value, and posts ONLY the genuinely high-value / novel ones as DRAFT external_finding entries. It is selective by design — most items are skipped. Phase 5: DRAFT only. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/workspace-example/scout) · ★1)
- **omoikane-scout**: Bring external findings (papers, posts, code, runtime telemetry) inside on a heartbeat. Correlate them against existing entries and propose ingest as new entries or as supplements. Phase 5: drafts only. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/scout) · ★1)
- **omoikane-summarizer**: Summarizer librarian for the omoikane Agent Knowledge Base. Each morning it distils the PREVIOUS day across omoikane — scout's external findings, new knowledge (traps/lessons/decisions/incidents/ design), and librarian activity — into ONE readable daily journal, posted ACTIVE so a human can read and search it immediately. ([zenryokukikai/omoikane](https://github.com/zenryokukikai/omoikane/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/librarians/workspace-example/summarizer) · ★1)
- **omoikane-summarizer**: Close chat threads when end-conditions fire; produce thread summaries that other librarians and humans can consult later. - **omoikane-synthesizer**: Distil the COMMON insight across the entries of a mature UseCase into one project-agnostic takeaway.
